          } else {
            if (d.value.case === "chatMessage") {
              this.handleChatMessage(p, d.value.value);
            } else {
              if (d.value.case === "metrics") {
                this.handleMetrics(d.value.value, p);
              } else {
                if (d.value.case === "streamHeader" || d.value.case === "streamChunk" || d.value.case === "streamTrailer") {
                  this.handleDataStream(d, h);
                } else {
                  if (d.value.case === "rpcRequest") {
                    const A = d.value.value;
                    this.rpcServerManager.handleIncomingRpcRequest(d.participantIdentity, A);
                  } else {
                    if (d.value.case === "rpcResponse") {
                      const A = d.value.value;
                      switch (A.value.case) {
                        case "payload":
                          this.rpcClientManager.handleIncomingRpcResponseSuccess(A.requestId, A.value.value);
                          break;
                        case "error":
                          this.rpcClientManager.handleIncomingRpcResponseFailure(A.requestId, Yl.fromProto(A.value.value));
                          break;
                        default:
                          this.log.warn("Unknown rpcResponse.value.case: ".concat(A.value.case), this.logContext);
                      }
                    } else {
                      d.value.case === "rpcAck" && this.rpcClientManager.handleIncomingRpcAck(d.value.value.requestId);
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    };
    this.handleUserPacket = (d, h, p, A) => {
      this.emit(En.DataReceived, h.payload, d, p, h.topic, A);
      d?.emit(Kn.DataReceived, h.payload, p, A);

  prepareConnection(t, n) {
    return Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
      if (this.state === Gs.Disconnected) {
        this.log.debug("prepareConnection to ".concat(t));
        try {
          if (Ow(new URL(t)) && n) {
            this.regionUrlProvider = new my(t, n);
            const i = yield this.regionUrlProvider.getNextBestRegionUrl();
            i && this.state === Gs.Disconnected && (this.regionUrl = i, yield fetch(lT(i), {
              method: "HEAD"
            }), this.log.debug("prepared connection to ".concat(i)));
          } else {
            yield fetch(lT(t), {
              method: "HEAD"
            });
          }
        } catch (i) {
          this.log.warn("could not prepare connection", {
            error: i
          });
        }
      }
    });
  }

  simulateScenario(t, n) {
    return Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
      let i = () => Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {});
      let r;
      switch (t) {
        case "signal-reconnect":
          yield this.engine.client.handleOnClose("simulate disconnect");
          break;
        case "fail-on-v1-path":
          this.engine.failNextV1Path();
          break;
        case "speaker":
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "speakerUpdate",
              value: 3
            }
          });
          break;
        case "node-failure":
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "nodeFailure",
              value: !0
            }
          });
          break;
        case "server-leave":
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "serverLeave",
              value: !0
            }
          });
          break;
        case "migration":
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "migration",
              value: !0
            }
          });
          break;
        case "resume-reconnect":
          this.engine.failNext();
          yield this.engine.client.handleOnClose("simulate resume-disconnect");
          break;
        case "disconnect-signal-on-resume":
          i = () => Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
            yield this.engine.client.handleOnClose("simulate resume-disconnect");
          });
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "disconnectSignalOnResume",
              value: !0
            }
          });
          break;
        case "disconnect-signal-on-resume-no-messages":
          i = () => Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
            yield this.engine.client.handleOnClose("simulate resume-disconnect");
          });
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "disconnectSignalOnResumeNoMessages",
              value: !0
            }
          });
          break;
        case "full-reconnect":
          this.engine.fullReconnectOnNext = !0;
          yield this.engine.client.handleOnClose("simulate full-reconnect");
          break;
        case "force-tcp":
        case "force-tls":
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "switchCandidateProtocol",
              value: t === "force-tls" ? 2 : 1
            }
          });
          i = () => Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
            const s = this.engine.client.onLeave;
            s && s(new vD({
              reason: Ip.CLIENT_INITIATED,
              action: zS.RECONNECT
            }));
          });
          break;
        case "subscriber-bandwidth":
          if (n === void 0 || typeof n != "number") {
            throw new Error("subscriber-bandwidth requires a number as argument");
          }
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "subscriberBandwidth",
              value: lm(n)
            }
          });
          break;
        case "leave-full-reconnect":
          r = new p0({
            scenario: {
              case: "leaveRequestFullReconnect",
              value: !0
            }
          });
      }
      r && (yield this.engine.client.sendSimulateScenario(r), yield i());
    });
  }

  onTrackAdded(t, n, i) {
    var r;
    var s;
    if ([Gs.Connecting, Gs.Reconnecting].includes(this.state)) {
      const A = zY(t, n);
      this.log.debug("deferring on track for later", {
        mediaTrackId: t.id,
        mediaStreamId: n.id,
        tracksInStream: n.getTracks().map(b => b.id)
      });
      const y = () => {
        E();
        this.onTrackAdded(t, n, i);
      };
      const E = () => {
        this.off(En.Reconnected, y);
        this.off(En.Connected, y);
        this.off(En.Disconnected, E);
        if (A) {
          const b = this.pendingTrackAddedCallbacks.get(A);
          b?.delete(E);
          b?.size === 0 && this.pendingTrackAddedCallbacks.delete(A);
        }
      };
      this.once(En.Reconnected, y);
      this.once(En.Connected, y);
      this.once(En.Disconnected, E);
      if (A) {
        const b = (r = this.pendingTrackAddedCallbacks.get(A)) !== null && r !== void 0 ? r : new Set();
        b.add(E);
        this.pendingTrackAddedCallbacks.set(A, b);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (this.state === Gs.Disconnected) {
      this.log.warn("skipping incoming track after Room disconnected");
      return;
    }
    if (t.readyState === "ended") {
      this.log.debug("skipping incoming track as it already ended");
      return;
    }

    const a = o[0];
    const l = o[1];

    }

      return;
    }

      const A = this.engine.getTrackIdForReceiver(i);
      if (!A) {
        this.log.error("Tried to add a track whose 'sid' could not be found for a participant, that's not present. Sid: ".concat(a));
        return;
      }
      u = A;
    }

      remoteParticipantID: a,
      streamId: l,
      trackId: u
    });

  }

(function (e) {
  e[e.IDLE = 0] = "IDLE";

var TE = class extends zf.EventEmitter {
  constructor(e, t) {
    let n = arguments.length > 2 && arguments[2] !== void 0 ? arguments[2] : {};
    super();
    this.status = XA.IDLE;
    this.logs = [];
    this.options = {};
    this.url = e;
    this.token = t;
    this.name = this.constructor.name;
    this.room = new fT(n.roomOptions);
    this.connectOptions = n.connectOptions;
    this.options = n;
  }
  run(e) {
    return Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
      if (this.status !== XA.IDLE) {
        throw Error("check is running already");
      }
      this.setStatus(XA.RUNNING);
      try {
        yield this.perform();
      } catch (t) {
        t instanceof Error && (this.options.errorsAsWarnings ? this.appendWarning(t.message) : this.appendError(t.message));
      }
      yield this.disconnect();
      yield new Promise(t => setTimeout(t, 500));
      this.status !== XA.SKIPPED && this.setStatus(this.isSuccess() ? XA.SUCCESS : XA.FAILED);
      e && e();
      return this.getInfo();
    });
  }
  isSuccess() {
    return !this.logs.some(e => e.level === "error");
  }
  connect(e) {
    return Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
      return this.room.state === Gs.Connected ? this.room : (e || (e = this.url), yield this.room.connect(e, this.token, this.connectOptions), this.room);
    });
  }
  disconnect() {
    return Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
      this.room && this.room.state !== Gs.Disconnected && (yield this.room.disconnect(), yield new Promise(e => setTimeout(e, 500)));
    });
  }
  skip() {
    this.setStatus(XA.SKIPPED);
  }
  switchProtocol(e) {
    return Ge(this, void 0, void 0, function* () {
      let t = !1;
      let n = !1;
      this.room.on(En.Reconnecting, () => {
        t = !0;
      });
      this.room.once(En.Reconnected, () => {
        n = !0;
      });
      this.room.simulateScenario("force-".concat(e));
      yield new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1e3));
      if (!t) {
        return;
      }
      const i = Date.now() + 1e4;
      for (; Date.now() < i;) {
        if (n) {
          return;
        }
        yield lh(100);
      }
      throw new Error("Could not reconnect using ".concat(e, " protocol after 10 seconds"));
    });
  }
  appendMessage(e) {
    this.logs.push({
      level: "info",
      message: e
    });
    this.emit("update", this.getInfo());
  }
  appendWarning(e) {
    this.logs.push({
      level: "warning",
      message: e
    });
    this.emit("update", this.getInfo());
  }
  appendError(e) {
    this.logs.push({
      level: "error",
      message: e
    });
    this.emit("update", this.getInfo());
  }
  setStatus(e) {
    this.status = e;
    this.emit("update", this.getInfo());
  }
  get engine() {
    var e;
    return (e = this.room) === null || e === void 0 ? void 0 : e.engine;
  }
  getInfo() {
    return {
      logs: this.logs,
      name: this.name,
      status: this.status,
      description: this.description
    };
  }
};

function eJe(e) {
  const t = e.split(".");
  if (t.length === 4) {
    if (t[0] === "10") {
      return !0;
    }
    if (t[0] === "192" && t[1] === "168") {
      return !0;
    }
    if (t[0] === "172") {
      const n = parseInt(t[1], 10);
      if (n >= 16 && n <= 31) {
        return !0;
      }
    }
  }
  return !1;
}
